```java
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

```java
public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;

        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();

        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;

        }
```

```java
public class AllUnique {
    public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null) return true;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}
```

```java
//@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
```
```java
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == true || \result == false;
    //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
```

```java
    public class AllUnique {
```
```java
        //@ requires arr != null;
        //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
```
```java
        public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
            if (arr == null) return true;
```

```java
            //@ maintaining (\forall int k, int l; 0 <= k < i && 0 <= l < i; arr[k] != arr[l]);
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
                //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 0 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);
                for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                    if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
```

```java
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == true || \result == false;
    //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;

    public class AllUnique {
        //@ requires arr != null;
        //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == \old(arr) != null ==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < \old(arr.length) && 0 <= j < \old(arr.length) && i != j; \old(arr[i]) != \old(arr[j]));
        public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
            if (arr == null) return true;

            //@ maintaining (\forall int k, int l; 0 <= k < i && 0 <= l < i; arr[k] != arr[l]);
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
                //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 0 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);
                for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                    if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
```

```java
//@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == true || \result == false;
    //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;

    public class AllUnique {
        //@ requires arr != null;
        //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == \old(arr) != null ==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < \old(arr.length) && 0 <= j < \old(arr.length) && i != j; \old(arr[i]) != \old(arr[j]));
        public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
            if (arr == null) return true;

            //@ maintaining (\forall int k, int l; 0 <= k < i && 0 <= l < i; arr[k] != arr[l]);
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
                //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 0 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);
                for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                    if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
```

```java
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == true || \result == false;
    //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;

    public class AllUnique {
        //@ requires arr != null;
        //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == \old(arr) != null ==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < \old(arr.length) && 0 <= j < \old(arr.length) && i != j; \old(arr[i]) != \old(arr[j]));
        public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
            if (arr == null) return true;

            //@ maintaining (\forall int k, int l; 0 <= k < i && 0 <= l < i; arr[k] != arr[l]);
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
                //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 0 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);
                for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                    if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
```

```java
//@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == true || \result == false;
    //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;

    public class AllUnique {
        //@ requires arr != null;
        //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == \old(arr) != null ==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < \old(arr.length) && 0 <= j < \old(arr.length) && i != j; \old(arr[i]) != \old(arr[j]));
        public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
            if (arr == null) return true;

            //@ maintaining (\forall int k, int l; 0 <= k < i && 0 <= l < i; arr[k] != arr[l]);
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
                //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 0 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);
                for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                    if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
```

```java
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == true || \result == false;
    //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;

    public class AllUnique {
        //@ requires arr != null;
        //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == \old(arr) != null ==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < \old(arr.length) && 0 <= j < \old(arr.length) && i != j; \old(arr[i]) != \old(arr[j]));
        public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
            if (arr == null) return true;

            //@ maintaining (\forall int k, int l; 0 <= k < i && 0 <= l < i; arr[k] != arr[l]);
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
                //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 0 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);
                for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                    if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
```

```java
//@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == true || \result == false;
    //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;

    public class AllUnique {
        //@ requires arr != null;
        //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == \old(arr) != null ==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < \old(arr.length) && 0 <= j < \old(arr.length) && i != j; \old(arr[i]) != \old(arr[j]));
        public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
            if (arr == null) return true;

            //@ maintaining (\forall int k, int l; 0 <= k < i && 0 <= l < i; arr[k] != arr[l]);
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
                //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 0 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);
                for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                    if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
```

```java
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == true || \result == false;
    //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;

    public class AllUnique {
        //@ requires arr != null;
        //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == \old(arr) != null ==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < \old(arr.length) && 0 <= j < \old(arr.length) && i != j; \old(arr[i]) != \old(arr[j]));
        public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
            if (arr == null) return true;

            //@ maintaining (\forall int k, int l; 0 <= k < i && 0 <= l < i; arr[k] != arr[l]);
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
                //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 0 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);
                for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                    if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
```

```java
//@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == true || \result == false;
    //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;

    public class AllUnique {
        //@ requires arr != null;
        //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == \old(arr) != null ==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < \old(arr.length) && 0 <= j < \old(arr.length) && i != j; \old(arr[i]) != \old(arr[j]));
        public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
            if (arr == null) return true;

            //@ maintaining (\forall int k, int l; 0 <= k < i && 0 <= l < i; arr[k] != arr[l]);
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
                //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 0 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);
                for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                    if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
```

```java
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == true || \result == false;
    //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;

    public class AllUnique {
        //@ requires arr != null;
        //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == \old(arr) != null ==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < \old(arr.length) && 0 <= j < \old(arr.length) && i != j; \old(arr[i]) != \old(arr[j]));
        public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
            if (arr == null) return true;

            //@ maintaining (\forall int k, int l; 0 <= k < i && 0 <= l < i; arr[k] != arr[l]);
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
                //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 0 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);
                for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                    if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
```

```java
//@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == true || \result == false;
    //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;

    public class AllUnique {
        //@ requires arr != null;
        //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == \old(arr) != null ==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < \old(arr.length) && 0 <= j < \old(arr.length) && i != j; \old(arr[i]) != \old(arr[j]));
        public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
            if (arr == null) return true;

            //@ maintaining (\forall int k, int l; 0 <= k < i && 0 <= l < i; arr[k] != arr[l]);
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
                //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 0 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);
                for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                    if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
```

```java
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == true || \result == false;
    //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;

    public class AllUnique {
        //@ requires arr != null;
        //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == \old(arr) != null ==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < \old(arr.length) && 0 <= j < \old(arr.length) && i != j; \old(arr[i]) != \old(arr[j]));
        public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
            if (arr == null) return true;

            //@ maintaining (\forall int k, int l; 0 <= k < i && 0 <= l < i; arr[k] != arr[l]);
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
                //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 0 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);
                for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                    if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
```

```java
//@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == true || \result == false;
    //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
    //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;

    public class AllUnique {
        //@ requires arr != null;
        //@ ensures \result == true <==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == false <==> (\exists int i, int j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 <= j < arr.length && i != j; arr[i] == arr[j]);
        //@ ensures \result == \old(arr) != null ==> (\forall int i, int j; 0 <= i < \old(arr.length) && 0 <= j < \old(arr.length) && i != j; \old(arr[i]) != \old(arr[j]));
        public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
            if (arr == null) return true;

            //@ maintaining (\forall int k, int l; 0 <= k < i && 0 <= l < i; arr[k] != arr[l]);
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
                //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 0 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);
                for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                    if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
```
